for

({\rm rem}_{11})^{\mu}=-\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\lambda}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\lambda}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\lambda}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\lambda}}}}\,T^{\lambda\nu}-\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\lambda}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\lambda}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\lambda}}}}{\Gamma^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\lambda}}}}\,T^{\mu\lambda}.

(2.20)

Now integration by parts gives

\displaystyle c^{-1}\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{0i}\,{\bar{x}}^{j}\,d\bar{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\partial_{\bar{x}^{0}}T^{0i})\,\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(-\partial_{\bar{x}^{m}}T^{mi}+({\rm rem}_{11})^{i})\,\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}

(2.21)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{ij}\,d\bar{x}+({\rm rem}_{12})^{ij},

where

({\rm rem}_{12})^{ij}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}({\rm rem}_{11})^{i}\,\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}.

(2.22)

With another application of (
2.19
), integration by parts
and (
2.21
), we are led to

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{d}^{\,ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\bar{x}^{0}}T^{00}\,\bar{x}^{i}\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}=c\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(-\partial_{\bar{x}^{m}}T^{m0})\,\bar{x}^{i}\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}+({\rm rem}_{13})^{ij}

(2.23)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(T^{j0}\bar{x}^{i}+T^{i0}\bar{x}^{j})\,d\bar{x}+({\rm rem}_{13})^{ij}=2c^{2}\tilde{a}^{\,ij}+({\rm rem}_{14})^{ij},

denoting

({\rm rem}_{13})^{ij}=c\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{11})^{0}\,\bar{x}^{i}\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x},\quad({\rm rem}_{14})^{ij}=c^{2}(({\rm rem}_{12})^{ij}+({\rm rem}_{12})^{ji})+({\rm rem}_{13})^{ij}.

(2.24)

Hence

\displaystyle a_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{ij}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{i\alpha}g_{j\beta}T^{\alpha\beta}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta_{i\alpha}+h_{i\alpha})\,(\eta_{j\beta}+h_{j\beta})\,T^{\alpha\beta}\,d\bar{x}

(2.25)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{ij}\,d\bar{x}+({\rm rem}_{15})_{ij}=\tilde{a}^{\,ij}+({\rm rem}_{15})_{ij}=\frac{1}{2c^{2}}\,\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{d}^{\,ij}+({\rm rem}_{16})_{ij}

for

\displaystyle({\rm rem}_{15})_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta_{i\alpha}h_{j\beta}+h_{i\alpha}\eta_{j\beta}+h_{i\alpha}h_{j\beta})\,T^{\alpha\beta}\,d\bar{x},

(2.26)

\displaystyle({\rm rem}_{16})_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2c^{2}}\,({\rm rem}_{14})^{ij}+({\rm rem}_{15})_{ij}.

(2.27)

Now

\displaystyle\tilde{d}^{\,ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{00}\bar{x}^{i}\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g^{0\alpha}g^{0\beta}T_{\alpha\beta}\,\bar{x}^{i}\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta^{0\alpha}+H^{0\alpha})(\eta^{0\beta}+H^{0\beta})\,T_{\alpha\beta}\,\bar{x}^{i}\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}

(2.28)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle d^{\,ij}+({\rm rem}_{17})^{ij},

where

({\rm rem}_{17})^{ij}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta^{0\alpha}H^{0\beta}+H^{0\alpha}\eta^{0\beta}+H^{0\alpha}H^{0\beta})\,T_{\alpha\beta}\,\bar{x}^{i}\bar{x}^{j}\,d\bar{x}.

(2.29)

Going back to (
2.25
), we obtain

a_{ij}=\frac{1}{2c^{2}}\,\ddot{d}^{\,ij}+({\rm rem}_{18})_{ij}\quad\mbox{for}\quad({\rm rem}_{18})_{ij}=\frac{1}{2c^{2}}\,\partial_{t}^{2}({\rm rem}_{17})^{ij}+({\rm rem}_{16})_{ij}.

(2.30)

Also by (
2.19
) and Gauss’s Theorem

\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{0\nu}\,d\bar{x}=c\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\bar{x}^{0}}T^{0\nu}\,d\bar{x}=c\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(-\partial_{x^{m}}T^{m\nu}+({\rm rem}_{11})^{\nu})\,d\bar{x}=({\rm rem}_{19})^{\nu},

(2.31)

defining

({\rm rem}_{19})^{\nu}=c\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}({\rm rem}_{11})^{\nu}\,d\bar{x}

(2.32)

According to (
2.25
) we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{0\nu}\,d\bar{x}=a_{0\nu}=\tilde{a}^{\,0\nu}+({\rm rem}_{15})_{0\nu}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{0\nu}\,d\bar{x}+({\rm rem}_{15})_{0\nu},

so that in particular

\dot{m}=\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{00}\,d\bar{x}={\rm rem}_{20}

(2.33)

for

{\rm rem}_{20}=({\rm rem}_{19})^{0}+\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{15})_{00},

(2.34)

and in addition

\dot{p}_{j}=-\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{0j}\,d\bar{x}=({\rm rem}_{21})^{j}

(2.35)

for

({\rm rem}_{21})^{j}=-({\rm rem}_{19})^{j}-\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{15})_{0j}.

(2.36)

The next terms to consider are the
d^{\,i}
.
First we deduce from (
2.21
) and (
2.31
) that

\partial_{t}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{00}\,{\bar{x}}^{i}\,d\bar{x}=c\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{0i}\,d\bar{x}+c\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{12})^{0i}=({\rm rem}_{22})^{i},

where

({\rm rem}_{22})^{i}=c({\rm rem}_{19})^{i}+c\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{12})^{0i}.

(2.37)

Also

\displaystyle d^{\,i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{00}\,{\bar{x}}^{i}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{0\alpha}g_{0\beta}\,T^{\alpha\beta}\,{\bar{x}}^{i}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta_{0\alpha}+h_{0\alpha})(\eta_{0\beta}+h_{0\beta})\,T^{\alpha\beta}\,{\bar{x}}^{i}\,d\bar{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{00}\,{\bar{x}}^{i}\,d\bar{x}+({\rm rem}_{23})^{i}

for

({\rm rem}_{23})^{i}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta_{0\alpha}h_{0\beta}+h_{0\alpha}\eta_{0\beta}+h_{0\alpha}h_{0\beta})\,T^{\alpha\beta}\,{\bar{x}}^{i}\,d\bar{x},

(2.38)

and accordingly

\ddot{d}^{\,i}=({\rm rem}_{24})^{i},

(2.39)

where

({\rm rem}_{24})^{i}=({\rm rem}_{22})^{i}+\partial_{t}^{2}({\rm rem}_{23})^{i}.

(2.40)

Finally, from (
2.21
) we infer that

\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(T^{0m}\,{\bar{x}}^{j}-T^{0j}\,{\bar{x}}^{m})\,d\bar{x}=c({\rm rem}_{12})^{mj}-c({\rm rem}_{12})^{jm}.

(2.41)

As in (
2.23
),

c^{-1}\partial_{t}\,\tilde{d}^{\,jm}=c^{-1}\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{00}\,\bar{x}^{j}\,\bar{x}^{m}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(T^{m0}\,\bar{x}^{j}+T^{j0}\,\bar{x}^{m})\,d\bar{x}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}({\rm rem}_{11})^{0}\,\bar{x}^{j}\,\bar{x}^{m}\,d\bar{x},

and this together with (
2.28
) leads to

\displaystyle c^{-1}\dot{d}^{\,jm}=c^{-1}\partial_{t}\,(\tilde{d}^{\,jm}-({\rm rem}_{17})^{jm})=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(T^{m0}\,\bar{x}^{j}+T^{j0}\,\bar{x}^{m})\,d\bar{x}+({\rm rem}_{25})^{jm},

for

({\rm rem}_{25})^{jm}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}({\rm rem}_{11})^{0}\,\bar{x}^{j}\,\bar{x}^{m}\,d\bar{x}-c^{-1}\partial_{t}\,({\rm rem}_{17})^{jm}.

(2.42)

Hence (
2.41
) implies

\displaystyle c^{-1}\ddot{d}^{\,jm}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(T^{m0}\,\bar{x}^{j}+T^{j0}\,\bar{x}^{m})\,d\bar{x}+\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{25})^{jm}

(2.43)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{0j}\,{\bar{x}}^{m}\,d\bar{x}+({\rm rem}_{26})^{jm},

defining

({\rm rem}_{26})^{jm}=c({\rm rem}_{12})^{mj}-c({\rm rem}_{12})^{jm}+\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{25})^{jm}.

(2.44)

Now

\displaystyle\mathchoice{b^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{b^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{b^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{b^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{0j}\,\bar{x}^{m}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}g_{\alpha 0}\,g_{\beta j}\,T^{\alpha\beta}\,\bar{x}^{m}\,d\bar{x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta_{\alpha 0}+h_{\alpha 0})\,(\eta_{\beta j}+h_{\beta j})\,T^{\alpha\beta}\,\bar{x}^{m}\,d\bar{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T^{0j}\,\bar{x}^{m}\,d\bar{x}+\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}},

where

\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\eta_{\alpha 0}h_{\beta j}+h_{\alpha 0}\eta_{\beta j}+h_{\alpha 0}h_{\beta j})\,T^{\alpha\beta}\,\bar{x}^{m}\,d\bar{x}.

(2.45)

Thus we get from (
2.43
) that

\frac{1}{2c}\,\ddot{d}^{\,jm}=-\mathchoice{\dot{b}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dot{b}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dot{b}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dot{b}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\partial_{t}\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\frac{1}{2}\,({\rm rem}_{26})^{jm},

and in particular

\frac{1}{2c}\,\dddot{d}^{\,jm}=-\mathchoice{\ddot{b}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\ddot{b}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\ddot{b}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\ddot{b}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+({\rm rem}_{28})^{jm}

(2.46)

for

({\rm rem}_{28})^{jm}=\partial_{t}^{2}\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{26})^{jm}.

(2.47)

2.5
Derivatives of weak-field coefficients

In order to evaluate the expression (
2.16
) we will need the derivatives

\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}
. Hence our next step is
to solve for
\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}
from the Einstein equations (
1.1
),
which, owing to (
2.6
) and (
1.19
), are written
in the Gaussian coordinates
{(x^{\mu})}_{\mu=0,1,2,3}
as

-\frac{1}{2}\,\Box\,\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}+{({\rm rem}_{4})}_{\alpha\beta}=G_{\alpha\beta}=\frac{8\pi G}{c^{4}}\,T_{\alpha\beta}

(2.48)

for
\Box=\eta^{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}}^{2}
. Identifying

T_{\alpha\beta}(x^{\mu})
with
T_{\alpha\beta}(t,x^{a})
[cf. the remark concerning
(
1.11
)] and
\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}(x^{\mu})
with
\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x^{a})
, they turn into

-\frac{1}{2}\,\Big(c^{-2}\partial_{t}^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{3}\partial_{x^{a}}^{2}\Big)\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x)+{({\rm rem}_{4})}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x)=\frac{8\pi G}{c^{4}}\,T_{\alpha\beta}(t,x).

(2.49)

After moving the
({\rm rem}_{4})
term to the right, the solution to the wave equation (
2.49
)
that reflects a situation where no radiation comes in from spatial infinity is found
by a spatial integral involving the source evaluated at the
retarded time
,
that is,

\displaystyle\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4G}{c^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|}\,T_{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x}

(2.50)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\pi}\,\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|}{({\rm rem}_{4})}_{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x}.

The integral containing the matter terms
T_{\alpha\beta}
in (
2.50
)
can be reduced to the bounded domain
\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}:\ |x|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}\}

due to the compact-support condition (
1.25
) on
T_{\alpha\beta}
.
Similarly, other
d\bar{x}
-integrals of matter terms that appear in what follows
only need to be performed over a bounded ball. However, the finiteness
of the second integral in (
2.50
), namely

{({\rm rem}_{29})}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x)=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\,\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|}{({\rm rem}_{4})}_{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x},

(2.51)

depends crucially on the NIR condition (
1.23
), reminiscent of Blanchet’s
no-incoming-radiation condition. In general (
2.51
) will be infinite
(cf.
[
17
, p. 4814]
for instance, and also Section
C
),
and several ways to remedy this problem
have been suggested, like the tail-integral approach or the Will-Wiseman
formalism
[
17
,
11
]
. However, we found condition (
1.23
)
to be the most analytically tractable. One may consult Appendix
C

for a more in-depth discussion, and also Subsection
3.5
,
where we will in particular see that
({\rm rem}_{29})
is finite.

It follows that

\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x)=\frac{4G}{c^{4}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|}\,T_{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x}+{({\rm rem}_{29})}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x).

(2.52)

We now find the derivatives of
\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}
. To begin with, from (
1.21
) we obtain

\displaystyle\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\eta^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4G}{c^{4}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|}\,\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x}+{({\rm rem}_{30})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

for

\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}=\eta^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\eta^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,T_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad{({\rm rem}_{30})}^{\alpha\beta}=\eta^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\eta^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}{({\rm rem}_{29})}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}.

(2.53)

Therefore

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{0}}\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c^{-1}\partial_{t}\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

(2.54)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|}\,\partial_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x}+{({\rm rem}_{31})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x),\qquad

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{k}}\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\frac{x^{k}}{|x-\bar{x}|^{2}}\,\partial_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x}+\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm (2.55)

defining

\displaystyle{({\rm rem}_{31})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c^{-1}\partial_{t}{({\rm rem}_{30})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x),

(2.56)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm \displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{4G}{c^{4}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\frac{x^{k}}{|x-\bar{x}|^{3}}\,\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x}+\partial_{x^{k}}{({\rm rem}_{30})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x).\qquad

(2.57)

Next we are going to use Lemma
5.12
(b) and (c)
from Appendix

to expand the right-hand sides
of (
2.54
) and (
2.55
), letting
r_{\ast}=\lambda K_{\ast}
in the notation
of that Lemma. It follows that, for
|u|\leq\lambda c^{-1}
and

,

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{0}}\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\,\bigg[\frac{1}{r}\,\partial_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})+\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})}{cr^{2}}\,\partial^{2}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt+\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})^{2}}{2c^{2}r^{3}}\,\partial^{3}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})\bigg]\,d\bar{x}+{({\rm rem}_{33})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

for

{({\rm rem}_{33})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)={({\rm rem}_{31})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)+{\cal O}\Big(c^{-5}r^{-2}\sum_{j=0}^{2}c^{-j}{\|\partial_{t}^{j+1}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{\infty}+c^{-8}r^{-1}{\|\partial_{t}^{4}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{\infty}\Big)

(2.58)

and

. Similarly,

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0001pt+\,x^{k}\,\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})^{2}}{2c^{2}r^{4}}\,\partial^{3}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})\bigg]\,d\bar{x}+\mathchoice{({\rm O}\Big(c^{-5}r^{-2}\sum_{j=0}^{2}c^{-j}{\|\partial_{t}^{j+1}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{\infty}+c^{-8}r^{-1}{\|\partial_{t}^{4}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{\infty}\Big).

(2.59)

To further simplify the right-hand sides of the
\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}
,
recall the auxiliary functions that have been defined in (
2.17
) and (
2.18
).
Since
\hat{T}^{00}=T_{00}
, it follows that

\partial_{x^{0}}\bar{h}^{00}(t,x)=\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\bigg[\frac{1}{r}\,\dot{m}(u)+\frac{1}{cr^{2}}\,\sum_{i=1}^{3}x^{i}\ddot{d}^{\,i}(u)+\frac{1}{2c^{2}r^{3}}\,\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}x^{i}x^{j}\dddot{d}^{ij}(u)\bigg]+{({\rm rem}_{33})}^{00}(t,x),

(2.60)

where
u=t-c^{-1}r
as before. Also
\hat{T}^{ij}=T_{ij}
implies that

\partial_{x^{0}}\bar{h}^{ij}(t,x)=\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\,\frac{1}{r}\,\dot{a}_{ij}(u)+{({\rm rem}_{35})}^{ij}(t,x)

(2.61)

for

{({\rm rem}_{35})}^{ij}(t,x)={({\rm rem}_{33})}^{ij}(t,x)+\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\,\bigg[\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})}{cr^{2}}\,\partial^{2}_{t}T_{ij}(u,\bar{x})+\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})^{2}}{2c^{2}r^{3}}\,\partial^{3}_{t}T_{ij}(u,\bar{x})\bigg]\,d\bar{x}.

(2.62)

Furthermore,

\partial_{x^{k}}\bar{h}^{00}(t,x)=-\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\bigg[\frac{x^{k}}{r^{2}}\,\dot{m}(u)+\frac{x^{k}}{cr^{3}}\,\sum_{i=1}^{3}x^{i}\ddot{d}^{\,i}(u)+\frac{x^{k}}{2c^{2}r^{4}}\,\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}x^{i}x^{j}\dddot{d}^{ij}(u)\bigg]+\mathchoice{({\rm (2.82)

2.6
Energy-rate formula

By (
1.20
) and (
1.21
),

\bar{h}=\eta^{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}=-\bar{h}_{00}+\bar{h}_{11}+\bar{h}_{22}+\bar{h}_{33}=-\bar{h}^{00}+\bar{h}^{11}+\bar{h}^{22}+\bar{h}^{33}.

In particular, denoting

\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(u)=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\dddot{D}^{\,jj}(u),

we get from (

)–(

)

\partial_{x^{0}}\bar{h}=-\frac{2G}{c^{5}r^{3}}\,\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}x^{i}x^{j}\dddot{D}^{\,ij}+\frac{2G}{c^{5}r}\,\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+({\rm rem}_{43}),

It follows that

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{0}}\bar{h}\,\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{x^{i}}{r}\,\partial_{x^{i}}\bar{h}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(-\frac{2G}{c^{5}r^{3}}\,\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}x^{i}x^{j}\dddot{D}^{\,ij}+\frac{2G}{c^{5}r}\,\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+({\rm rem}_{43})\Big)

(2.86)

Thus, for
|x|=r
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}|g|\,\frac{x^{i}}{r}\,t^{0i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{c^{4}}{16\pi G}\bigg[-\frac{2G^{2}}{c^{10}r^{6}}\,\sum_{k,l,m,n=1}^{3}x^{k}x^{l}x^{m}x^{n}\,\dddot{D}^{\,kl}\dddot{D}^{\,mn}+\sum_{i,j,k,m=1}^{3}\frac{4G^{2}x^{i}x^{j}x^{k}x^{m}}{c^{10}r^{6}}\,\dddot{D}^{\,im}\,\dddot{D}^{\,jk}

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt+\sum_{i,k,m,n=1}^{3}\frac{4G^{2}x^{i}x^{k}x^{m}x^{n}}{c^{10}r^{6}}\,\dddot{D}^{\,mn}\,\dddot{D}^{\,ik}-\sum_{i,j,k,m=1}^{3}\frac{4G^{2}x^{i}x^{j}x^{k}x^{m}}{c^{10}r^{6}}\,\dddot{D}^{\,jm}\,\dddot{D}^{\,ik}

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt+\sum_{j,k,m,n=1}^{3}\frac{4G^{2}x^{j}x^{k}x^{m}x^{n}}{c^{10}r^{6}}\,\dddot{D}^{\,jk}\,\dddot{D}^{\,mn}-\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\frac{4G^{2}x^{i}x^{k}}{c^{10}r^{4}}\,\dddot{D}^{\,jk}\,\dddot{D}^{\,ij}

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt-\sum_{j,k,m=1}^{3}\frac{4G^{2}x^{k}x^{m}}{c^{10}r^{4}}\,\dddot{D}^{\,jk}\,\dddot{D}^{\,jm}-\sum_{i,k,m,n=1}^{3}\frac{4G^{2}x^{i}x^{k}x^{m}x^{n}}{c^{10}r^{6}}\,\dddot{D}^{\,ik}\dddot{D}^{\,mn}

\displaystyle+\frac{c^{4}}{64\pi G}\,\partial_{x^{0}}\bar{h}\,\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{x^{i}}{r}\,\partial_{x^{i}}\bar{h}+({\rm rem}_{48})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{c^{4}}{16\pi G}\bigg[\frac{2G^{2}}{c^{10}r^{6}}\,\sum_{k,l,m,n=1}^{3}x^{k}x^{l}x^{m}x^{n}\,\dddot{D}^{\,kl}\dddot{D}^{\,mn}-\sum_{i,j,k=1}^{3}\frac{4G^{2}x^{i}x^{k}}{c^{10}r^{4}}\,\dddot{D}^{\,jk}\,\dddot{D}^{\,ij}

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt+\frac{2G^{2}}{c^{10}r^{2}}\,\sum_{j,k=1}^{3}(\dddot{D}^{\,jk})^{2}\bigg]+\frac{c^{4}}{64\pi G}\,\partial_{x^{0}}\bar{h}\,\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{x^{i}}{r}\,\partial_{x^{i}}\bar{h}+({\rm rem}_{48}),

defining

({\rm rem}_{48})=\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{x^{i}}{r}({\rm rem}_{47})^{i}.

Also observe the following simplification:

\displaystyle\Big(\dddot{D}_{jk}-\frac{1}{3}\,\eta_{jk}\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\Big)\Big(\dddot{D}^{\,jk}-\frac{1}{3}\,\eta^{jk}\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dddot{D}_{jk}\dddot{D}^{\,jk}-\frac{2}{3}\dddot{D}^{\,jk}\eta^{jk}\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\frac{1}{9}\,\eta_{jk}\,\eta^{jk}\,(\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{n}}}_{{{n}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.100 \displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k=1}^{3}(\dddot{D}^{\,jk})^{2}-\frac{1}{3}\,(\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})^{2},

if it is understood that the indices are raised and lowered using
\eta
.
By (
2.93
), re-inserting the correct function arguments, we finally arrive at

\bigg|\partial_{t}\,\mathfrak{p}^{0}(t,x)-\bigg(-\frac{G}{5c^{5}}\bigg[\sum_{j,k=1}^{3}(\dddot{D}^{\,jk}(u))^{2}-\frac{1}{3}(\mathchoice{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\dddot{D}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(u))^{2}\bigg]\bigg)\bigg|=|({\rm rem}_{50})(t,x)|.

(2.95)

To finish the proof of Theorem
1.2
, we now need to establish that
|({\rm rem}_{50})(t,x)|

can be bounded by the expression on the right-hand side of (
1.34
).
This will be achieved in (
3.74
) ahead.

3
Estimating the remainders

In this section we find estimates for all the remainder terms obtained in the above derivation
of the quadrupole formula. For clarity, the calculations are divided into subsections based
on the direct dependence between the corresponding remainder terms. Throughout our estimates,
we will denote by
C>0
universal constants that may change from line to line.

It should be mentioned that remainder 29 from (
2.51
)
is the most complicated one to control (which is no surprise,
see the discussion in Appendix
C
).
In fact at this point the precise decay estimates
on
h
and its derivatives are required, whereas for all of the other terms
simpler pointwise bounds would be sufficient.

3.1
Remainders 5 and 6: (
2.8
) and (
2.9
)

Recall that
\hat{h}=(h_{\alpha\beta})
and
h=\eta^{\alpha\beta}h_{\alpha\beta}
.
We can apply (
E.52
) from Lemma
5.5
(a)
with
n=4
and the matrix
\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}
to obtain the pointwise bound

|({\rm rem}_{5})|\leq C|\hat{h}|^{2},

(3.1)

where
|\hat{h}|
means
\max_{\alpha,\beta=0,1,2,3}|\hat{h}_{\alpha\beta}|

pointwise in
(t,x)
, with the arguments
(t,x)
of
({\rm rem}_{5})
and
\hat{h}

having been suppressed for brevity; such abbreviations will be used throughout this entire section.
Therefore Lemma
5.5
(b) with
x=h+({\rm rem}_{5})
yields

|({\rm rem}_{6})|\leq C(h+({\rm rem}_{5}))^{2}\leq C(h^{2}+|\hat{h}|^{4})\leq C(|\hat{h}|^{2}+|\hat{h}|^{4})\leq C|\hat{h}|^{2}.

(3.2)

To bound the derivatives of these remainders, we use the Jacobi formula

\partial_{x^{\gamma}}(\det A)=(\det A)\,{\rm tr}\,(A^{-1}\partial_{x^{\gamma}}A),

valid for any invertible-matrix-valued function
A
. Using the notation from Section
E.1.4
and the function
A=I+\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}
, we have
A=\hat{m}^{-1}(\hat{m}+\hat{h})=\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{g}
, and hence
A^{-1}=\hat{g}^{-1}\hat{m}=(\hat{m}^{-1}+\hat{H})\hat{m}=I+\hat{H}\hat{m}
, where
\hat{H}=(H^{\alpha\beta})
. It follows that

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{5})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\Big(\det(I+\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h})-1-{\rm tr}\,(\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h})\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\det(I+\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h})\,{\rm tr}\Big((I+\hat{H}\hat{m})\hat{m}^{-1}\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\Big)-{\rm tr}\,(\hat{m}^{-1}\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\det(I+\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h})-1)\,{\rm tr}\Big((\hat{m}^{-1}+\hat{H})\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\Big)+{\rm tr}\,(\hat{H}\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}),

and thus from (
E.52
) in Lemma
5.5
(a) we find

|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{5})|\leq C(|\hat{h}|+|\hat{H}|)\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|,

(3.3)

Next we calculate

\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{6})=\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\,\Big(\sqrt{1+h+({\rm rem}_{5})}-1-\frac{1}{2}\,h\Big)=\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{\partial_{x^{\gamma}}h+\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{5})}{\sqrt{1+h+({\rm rem}_{5})}}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\gamma}}h.

Due to (
3.3
), Lemma
5.5
(b) and (
3.1
), we find the bound

\displaystyle|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{6})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{5})|+C|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}h|\,|\sqrt{1+h+({\rm rem}_{5})}-1|

(3.4)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|\hat{h}|+|\hat{H}|)\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+C(|h|+|\hat{h}|^{2})\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}h|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|\hat{h}|+|\hat{H}|)\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|.

3.2
Remainders 7, 8 and 9: (
2.11
), (
2.12
) and (
2.14
)

Using the notation of equation (
E.18
),
we have
\hat{g}\hat{H}\hat{m}=\hat{g}(\hat{g}^{-1}-\hat{m}^{-1})\hat{m}=-\hat{h}
,
and hence
\hat{H}=-\hat{g}^{-1}\hat{h}\hat{m}^{-1}
. Therefore

\displaystyle({\rm rem}_{7})^{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h^{\alpha\beta}+H^{\alpha\beta}=\eta^{\alpha\mu}h_{\mu\nu}\eta^{\nu\beta}+H^{\alpha\beta}=(\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}\,\hat{m}^{-1})^{\alpha\beta}+\hat{H}^{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big((\hat{m}^{-1}-\hat{g}^{-1})\hat{h}\,\hat{m}^{-1}\Big)^{\alpha\beta}=-(\hat{H}\hat{h}\hat{m}^{-1})^{\alpha\beta}\ ,

that is,

({\rm rem}_{7})^{\alpha\beta}=-\eta^{\nu\beta}h_{\mu\nu}H^{\alpha\mu}.

This yields the bound

|({\rm rem}_{7})|\leq C|\hat{h}||\hat{H}|.

(3.5)

Next, taking a derivative,

|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{7})|\leq C(|\hat{h}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}|+|\hat{H}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|).

(3.6)

In our derivation of the quadrupole formula in Section
2
, we don’t need any bounds for
({\rm rem}_{8})
itself, but rather only for its derivative, which is the definition of
({\rm rem}_{9})
:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{8})^{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\gamma}}h)h^{\alpha\beta}-\frac{1}{2}h(\partial_{x^{\gamma}}h^{\alpha\beta})+\big(\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{7})^{\alpha\beta}\big)\left(1+\frac{h}{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\,({\rm rem}_{7})^{\alpha\beta}\,(\partial_{x^{\gamma}}h)

\displaystyle+\,\big(\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{6})\big)(\eta^{\alpha\beta}-h^{\alpha\beta}+({\rm rem}_{7})^{\alpha\beta})+({\rm rem}_{6})(-\partial_{x^{\gamma}}h^{\alpha\beta}+\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{7})^{\alpha\beta})\ .

Thus it follows from

h^{\alpha\beta}=\eta^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\eta^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}h_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}

and (
3.6
), (
3.5
), (
3.4
), (
3.2
) that

\displaystyle|\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha\beta}}}_{{{\gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\,|\hat{h}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+C\,(|\hat{h}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}|+|\hat{H}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|)+C\,|\hat{h}||\hat{H}|\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|

(3.7)

\displaystyle+\,C(|\hat{h}|+|\hat{H}|)\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+C|\hat{h}|^{2}\Big(|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+|\hat{h}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}|+|\hat{H}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\,\Big(|\hat{h}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+|\hat{h}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}|+|\hat{H}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|\Big).

3.3
Remainder 10: (
2.15
)

To bound the lengthy remainder 10, we can use
\bar{h}^{\alpha\beta}=-h^{\alpha\beta}
,
(
E.16
), (
E.20
) and the estimate (
3.7
) for
({\rm rem}_{9})
just above. We obtain

\displaystyle|({\rm rem}_{10})^{\alpha\beta}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|\,|\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\delta}{\gamma}}}_{{{\delta}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{9})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\delta}{\gamma}}}_{{{\delta}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.63474pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17776pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55554pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.20052pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81206pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm 3.4
Remainders 18, 20, 21, 24, 28

Subsection
2.4
defined remainders 11 through 28, and those appearing
in the title of the present part, defined in equations (
2.30
), (
2.34
), (
2.36
),
(
2.40
) and (
2.47
), are the ones that will figure in the next part. Remainder 11
was defined as an expression involving
T^{\mu\nu}
and
\Gamma^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}
terms,
while remainders 15, 17, 23 and 27 were defined in (
2.26
), (
2.29
), (
2.38
)
and (
2.45
) as integrals of expressions involving these terms as well as
h_{\mu\nu}
terms.
All the other remainders between 11 and 28 were algebraic and/or derivative expressions of these.
Thus simple pointwise bounds depending on
\delta_{T}
and
\delta_{h}
terms can be obtained
for all these remainders one by one, starting with
({\rm rem}_{11})
and working our way upwards.

From (
E.21
) we recall that

\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\mu\nu}=\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\sigma\kappa}\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}h_{\nu\kappa}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}h_{\mu\kappa}-\partial_{x^{\kappa}}h_{\mu\nu}),

which together with (
E.20
) leads to

|\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\mu\nu}|\leq C|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|.

For the derivatives, by (
E.18
),

\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\mu\nu}=\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\gamma}}H^{\sigma\kappa}\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}h_{\nu\kappa}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}h_{\mu\kappa}-\partial_{x^{\kappa}}h_{\mu\nu})+\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\sigma\kappa}\,(\partial^{2}_{x^{\gamma}x^{\mu}}h_{\nu\kappa}+\partial^{2}_{x^{\gamma}x^{\nu}}h_{\mu\kappa}-\partial^{2}_{x^{\gamma}x^{\kappa}}h_{\mu\nu})

and hence

|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\mu\nu}|\leq C(|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|),

and similarly

|\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\gamma}}\Gamma^{\sigma}_{\mu\nu}|\leq C\Big(|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}||\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+|\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|\Big).

Using (
E.23
), we deduce that
({\rm rem}_{11})
as defined in (
2.20
) can be bounded as

|({\rm rem}_{11})^{\mu}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{2}}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|

(3.9)

and from

\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm also

\displaystyle|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{11})^{\mu}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}||\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|)\,\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{2}}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}+C|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|\,\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{3}}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})

(3.10)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\,\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{4}}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})\,(\lambda|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+\lambda^{2}|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|),

and similarly

|\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{11})^{\mu}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{5}}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)}+\delta_{T}^{(2)})\,\Big(\lambda|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+\lambda^{2}|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|+\lambda^{3}|\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}|\Big).

(3.11)

The compact-support condition (
1.25
) for
T^{\alpha\beta}
implies that the integral (
2.22
)
in the definition of
({\rm rem}_{12})
can be reduced to a ball of radius proportional to
\lambda

(the proportionality constant,
K_{\ast}
, will be incorporated into
C
). In particular
also the term
|\bar{x}^{j}|
inside that integral is bounded in this way, while (
3.9
)
gives an estimate for the rest of the integrand. Thus we find

|({\rm rem}_{12})^{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{4}\lambda^{2}}{G}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}\,{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

(3.12)

Similarly, differentiating under the integral and owing to (
3.10
),

|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}({\rm rem}_{12})^{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{4}}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})\,(\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}).

(3.13)

Analogue reasoning leads to the following for
({\rm rem}_{13})
as defined in (
2.24
)

|({\rm rem}_{13})^{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{6}\lambda}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})\,(\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}),

(3.14)

|\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{13})^{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{7}}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)}+\delta_{T}^{(2)})\,\Big(\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{3}{\|\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big),

(3.15)

and with these one immediately finds for
({\rm rem}_{14})
as defined in (
2.24
)

|({\rm rem}_{14})^{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{6}\lambda}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})\,(\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}),

(3.16)

|\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{14})^{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{7}}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)}+\delta_{T}^{(2)})\,\Big(\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{3}{\|\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big).

(3.17)

For the rest of the pertinent remainders here, we simply state the relevant results of the computation:

|({\rm rem}_{15})_{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{4}\lambda}{G}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}\,{\|\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}},

(3.18)

|\partial_{t}({\rm rem}_{15})_{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{5}}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})\,({\|\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}),

(3.19)

(3.20)

(3.21)

(3.22)

(3.23)

(3.24)

|\partial_{t}^{3}({\rm rem}_{17})^{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{7}}{G}\,\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{3}\delta_{T}^{(j)}\Big)\,\Big({\|\hat{H}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{3}{\|\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big),

(3.25)

|({\rm rem}_{18})_{ij}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{4}\lambda}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)}+\delta_{T}^{(2)})\,\Big(\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\lambda^{|\alpha|}{\|\partial^{\alpha}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\lambda^{|\alpha|}{\|\partial^{\alpha}\hat{H}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big),

(3.26)

(3.27)

|({\rm rem}_{19})^{\nu}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{5}\lambda}{G}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}\,{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}},

(3.28)

|{\rm rem}_{20}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{5}}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})\,({\|\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}),

(3.30)

(3.33)

(3.35)

|({\rm rem}_{25})^{jm}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{4}\lambda^{2}}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})\,\Big(\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+{\|\hat{H}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{H}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big),

(3.38)
