(3.40)

|\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{4}\lambda^{2}}{G}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}\,{\|\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}},

(3.41)

|\partial_{t}\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{5}\lambda}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)})\,({\|\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}),

(3.42)

|\partial_{t}^{2}\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{27})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{m}}}_{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{6}}{G}\,(\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\delta_{T}^{(1)}+\delta_{T}^{(2)})\,\Big({\|\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big),

(3.43)

|({\rm rem}_{28})^{jm}|\leq C\,\frac{c^{6}}{G}\,\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{3}\delta_{T}^{(j)}\Big)\,\Big(\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 3}\lambda^{|\alpha|}{\|\partial^{\alpha}\hat{h}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 3}\lambda^{|\alpha|}{\|\partial^{\alpha}\hat{H}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big).

(3.44)

3.5
Remainders 4 and 29: (
2.7
) and (
2.51
)

From (
2.7
) we recall that

\displaystyle{({\rm rem}_{4})}_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,\tilde{P}(g;\partial_{x^{\alpha}}h,\partial_{x^{\beta}}h)+\frac{1}{2}\,\tilde{Q}_{\alpha\beta}(g;\partial h,\partial h)-\frac{1}{2}\,H^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}x^{\beta^{\prime}}}\,h_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{4}\,(\eta^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}+H^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\bigg(\tilde{P}(g;\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}h,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}h)+\tilde{Q}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}(g;\partial h,\partial h)-H^{\gamma\delta}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\gamma}x^{\delta}}\,h_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\bigg)\,g_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4}\,\Big(H^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\Box\,h_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\Big)\,g_{\alpha\beta}+\frac{1}{4}\,(\Box\,h)\,h_{\alpha\beta}

for
\tilde{P}
and
\tilde{Q}
given by (
B.31
) and (
B.32
), respectively. Since
h=\eta^{\alpha\beta}h_{\alpha\beta}
and
\Box=\eta^{\alpha\beta}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}}
, we see that
{({\rm rem}_{4})}_{\alpha\beta}
is a sum of terms of the following form:

\phi(\partial_{x^{\mu}}h_{\alpha\beta})\,(\partial_{x^{\nu}}h_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}),\,\,\phi\,H^{\alpha\beta}\,(\partial^{2}_{{x^{\mu}}{x^{\nu}}}h_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}),\,\,\phi\,h_{\alpha\beta}\,(\partial^{2}_{{x^{\mu}}{x^{\nu}}}h^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}),

where
\phi=\phi(t,x)
is a function such that
\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\lambda^{|\alpha|}|\partial^{\alpha}\phi|\leq C
. Then (
2.51
) defines

{({\rm rem}_{29})}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x)=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\,\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|x-y|}\,{({\rm rem}_{4})}_{\alpha\beta}(t-|x-y|,y)\,dy.

It is here that we need the NIR condition (
1.23
). Assume, as in the statement of Theorem (
1.2
), that

|t-c^{-1}|x||\leq\lambda c^{-1},\quad|x|\geq\max\{12\lambda,c{\cal T},2{\cal S}\},\quad{\cal T}\geq 2\lambda c^{-1},\quad{\cal S}\geq\max\{2c{\cal T},6\lambda\}.

(3.45)

In addition, due to (
1.23
) and
\delta_{h}=C_{0}\varepsilon_{h}
we may assume that

\hat{h}(t,x)=p(x)\quad\mbox{for}\quad t\leq-{\cal T},\quad|\partial^{\kappa}p(x)|\leq\frac{1}{\lambda^{|\alpha|}}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}}{|x|^{1+|\kappa|}},\quad|x|\geq{\cal S},\,\,|\kappa|\leq 3;

(3.46)

recall that
\hat{h}=(h_{\alpha\beta})
as a matrix. We will now establish the following result.

Lemma 3.1

Let
\gamma_{0}\in[\frac{3}{4},1)
and
\delta_{h}>0

be such that
\gamma_{0}+\delta_{h}\leq 1
and let (
3.45
)
and (
3.46
) be satisfied. Then

\lambda\,\bigg|\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|x-y|}\,\zeta(t-c^{-1}|x-y|,y)\,dy\bigg|\leq\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}

for each
\alpha=0,1,2,3
, where
\zeta
can stand for any of the following expressions:

\displaystyle\zeta

\displaystyle=\phi(\partial_{x^{\mu}}h_{\alpha\beta})\,(\partial_{x^{\nu}}h_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}),

\displaystyle\zeta

\displaystyle=\phi\,H^{\alpha\beta}\,(\partial^{2}_{{x^{\mu}}{x^{\nu}}}h_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}),\ \text{or}

\displaystyle\zeta

\displaystyle=\phi\,h_{\alpha\beta}\,(\partial^{2}_{{x^{\mu}}{x^{\nu}}}h^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}),

with
\mu,\nu,\alpha,\beta,\alpha^{\prime},\beta^{\prime}=0,1,2,3

and
\phi
being a function such that
\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\lambda^{|\alpha|}|\partial^{\alpha}\phi|\leq C
. In particular,

\lambda\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}{({\rm rem}_{29})}_{\alpha\beta}(t,x)|\leq\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}},\quad\alpha,\beta,\gamma=0,1,2,3.

(3.47)

Proof :
Throughout this proof we will simply write

h
for any
h_{\alpha\beta}
and similarly
H
for any
H^{\alpha\beta}
.
Also for simplicity we will let
G=1
,
\lambda=1
and
c=1
for the argument,
so that in particular
x^{0}=ct=t
. We are going to apply Corollary
5.3
,
(
E.47
) and (
E.49
) to appropriate
combinations of terms, where we take
\gamma=\gamma_{0}
and
\rho=\delta=\delta_{h}
.

Case 1:

\zeta=(\partial_{x^{\mu}}h)(\partial_{x^{\nu}}h)
.
Let
\Psi=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h
and
\tilde{\Psi}=h
,
so that
\zeta=\Psi\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\Psi}
.
(i) assumptions for
I_{1}
: it follows from (
E.15
) that (
E.48
) holds.
(ii) assumptions for
I_{2}
: from (
3.46
) we deduce that

\Psi(t,x)=\partial_{x^{\mu}}p(x)
for
t\leq-{\cal T}
and furthermore

|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x^{\mu}}p(x)|\leq C\delta_{h}|x|^{-(2+|\alpha|)}

for
|x|\geq{\cal S}
and
|\alpha|\leq 2
.
Similarly,
\tilde{\Psi}(x)=p(x)
for
t\leq-{\cal T}
and in addition

|\partial^{\alpha}p(x)|\leq C\delta_{h}|x|^{-(1+|\alpha|)}
for
|x|\geq{\cal S}
and
|\alpha|\leq 2
.
Therefore the conditions (
E.2.2
) and (
E.2.2
) are verified.
(iii) assumptions for
I_{3}
: this requires a more careful analysis. Writing
h=h^{0}+h^{1}
,
we decompose

\zeta=\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}+\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}+\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}+\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1},

(3.48)

where

\psi^{0}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h^{0},\,\,\psi^{1}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h^{1},\,\,\tilde{\psi}^{0}=h^{0},\,\,\tilde{\psi}^{1}=h^{1}.

Now we consider each term in (
3.48
) separately
and check the assumptions that allow us to bound
I_{3}
.
(a) For
\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}
we will show that
(
E.31
)-(
E.32
) holds. In fact this is a direct consequence
of (
E.4
) and (
E.5
).
(b) For
\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}

we note that (
E.40
) is satisfied,
by (
E.11
).
(c) For
\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}

we remark that (
E.13
) yields (
E.40
).
(d) For
\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}
we have (
E.35
)
due to (
E.9
).

Case 2:

\zeta=h(\partial^{2}_{x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h)
.
We set
\Psi=h
and
\tilde{\Psi}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h

to get
\zeta=\Psi\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\Psi}
.
(i) assumptions for
I_{1}
: it follows from (
E.15
) that (
E.48
) holds.
(ii) assumptions for
I_{2}
: from (
3.46
) we deduce that

\Psi(t,x)=p(x)
for
t\leq-{\cal T}
and furthermore

|\partial^{\alpha}p(x)|\leq C\delta_{h}|x|^{-(1+|\alpha|)}

for
|x|\geq{\cal S}
and
|\alpha|\leq 2
.
Similarly,
\tilde{\Psi}(x)=\partial_{x^{\mu}}p(x)
for
t\leq-{\cal T}
and in addition

|\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{x^{\mu}}p(x)|\leq C\delta_{h}|x|^{-(2+|\alpha|)}

for
|x|\geq{\cal S}
and
|\alpha|\leq 2
.
Therefore the conditions (
E.2.2
) and (
E.2.2
) are verified.
(iii) assumptions for
I_{3}
: once again this requires a more careful analysis.
Here we split up

\zeta=\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}+\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}+\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}+\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1},

where

\psi^{0}=h^{0},\,\,\psi^{1}=h^{1},\,\,\tilde{\psi}^{0}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h^{0},\,\,\tilde{\psi}^{1}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h^{1}.

(a) For
\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}
we need to show that
(
E.31
)-(
E.32
) is verified. This is in fact due to (
E.6
) and (
E.7
).
(b) For
\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}
we can use (
E.12
)
to get (
E.40
).
(c) For
\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}
we obtain (
E.40
) from (
E.14
).
(d) For
\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}
we note that (
E.35
)
is a consequence of (
E.10
).

Case 3:

\zeta=H(\partial^{2}_{x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h)
.
First recall that
h=h_{\alpha\beta}
and
H=H^{\gamma\delta}
denote
one of the components of
h
and
H
, respectively.
For the bounds on
H
we can rely on Section
E.1.4
,
where we wrote
Z=\hat{H}\hat{m}
,
X=\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}

and
Y=(I+X)^{-1}=(I+\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h})^{-1}

for the corresponding matrices
\hat{H}
for
H
,
\hat{m}

for the Minkowski metric and
\hat{h}
for
h
. Then
Z=-YX
yields

\hat{H}=-YX\hat{m}^{-1}=-Y\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}\hat{m}^{-1},

(3.49)

For any derivative therefore

(\partial_{x^{\lambda}}\hat{H})=-(\partial_{x^{\lambda}}Y)\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}\hat{m}^{-1}-Y\hat{m}^{-1}(\partial_{x^{\lambda}}\hat{h})\hat{m}^{-1}.

(3.50)

From (
3.49
) and (
3.50
) together with Section
E.1.4

we deduce in particular that, symbolically,

|H|\leq C|h|\quad\mbox{and}\quad|\partial_{x^{\lambda}}H|\leq C(|h|+|\partial_{x^{\lambda}}h|)

(3.51)

holds. Now we set
\Psi=H
and
\tilde{\Psi}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h

to get
\zeta=\Psi\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\Psi}
.
(i) assumptions for
I_{1}
: from (
3.51
) and (
E.15
)
we find
|H|+|\partial_{x^{\lambda}}H|\leq C
.
Hence (
E.48
) follows from (
E.15
).
(ii) assumptions for
I_{2}
: for
\tilde{\Psi}
we have (
E.2.2
) with
m=1
by (
3.46
).
Concerning (
E.2.2
), since
Y
is only depending on
\hat{h}
, (
3.49
) shows
that the Blanchet condition (
3.46
) for
h
implies the Blanchet condition also for
H
;
symbolically, we may write
H=\frac{h}{1+h}
. Thus (
E.2.2
) is verified for
l=0
.
(iii) assumptions for
I_{3}
: we decompose

\zeta=\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}+\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}+\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}+\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1},

where

\psi^{0}=H^{0},\,\,\psi^{1}=H^{1},\,\,\tilde{\psi}^{0}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h^{0},\,\,\tilde{\psi}^{1}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}h^{1},

and we let
H^{0}
and
H^{1}
be defined via

\hat{H}^{0}=-Y\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}^{0}\hat{m}^{-1}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\hat{H}^{1}=-Y\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}^{1}\hat{m}^{-1};

note that
Y
still depends on
h=h^{0}+h^{1}
. Then

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\hat{H}^{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-Y\hat{m}^{-1}(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\hat{h}^{0})\hat{m}^{-1}-(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}Y)\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}^{0}\hat{m}^{-1},

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\hat{H}^{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-Y\hat{m}^{-1}(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\hat{h}^{1})\hat{m}^{-1}-(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}Y)\hat{m}^{-1}\hat{h}^{1}\hat{m}^{-1}.

(a) For
\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}
we need to show that
(
E.31
)–(
E.32
) is verified. Firstly,

|\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}|=|H^{0}\partial^{2}_{x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h^{0}|\leq|h^{0}\partial^{2}_{x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h^{0}|\leq C\delta_{h}^{2}\,{\bf 1}_{\{r\geq\frac{1}{2}(1+|t|)\}}\,\frac{1}{|x|^{4}},

by Section
E.1.4
and Section
E.1.1
. Secondly,

\displaystyle|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}(\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}(H^{0}\partial^{2}_{x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h^{0})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|H^{0}\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h^{0}|+|(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}H^{0})\partial^{2}_{x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h^{0}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|h^{0}||\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h^{0}|+C(|h^{0}|+|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}h^{0}|)|\partial^{2}_{x^{\mu}x^{\nu}}h^{0}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\delta_{h}^{2}\,{\bf 1}_{\{r\geq\frac{1}{2}(1+|t|)\}}\,\frac{1}{|x|^{4}},

again by Section
E.1.4
and Section
E.1.1
.
(b) For
\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}
we estimate

\displaystyle|\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}|+|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}(\psi^{0}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|H^{0}\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|+|H^{0}\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|+|(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}H^{0})\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|h^{0}||\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|+C|h^{0}||\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|

\displaystyle+\,C(|h^{0}|+|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}h^{0}|)|\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\delta_{h}^{2}\,{\bf 1}_{\{r\geq\frac{1}{2}(1+|t|)\}}\,\frac{(1+|t|)^{\delta_{h}}}{(1+|t|+|x|)(1+(|x|-|t|)_{+})^{\gamma_{0}}}\,\frac{1}{|x|},

by means of the bounds on
h^{0}
and
h^{1}
. Therefore (
E.40
) is verified.
(c) Similarly, for
\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}
we find

\displaystyle|\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0}|+|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}(\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{0})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|H^{1}\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{0}|+|H^{1}\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{0}|+|(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}H^{1})\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{0}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|h^{1}||\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{0}|+C|h^{1}||\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{0}|

\displaystyle+\,C(|h^{1}|+|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}h^{1}|)|\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}h^{0}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\delta_{h}^{2}\,{\bf 1}_{\{r\geq\frac{1}{2}(1+|t|)\}}\,\frac{(1+|t|)^{\delta_{h}}}{(1+|t|+|x|)(1+(|x|-|t|)_{+})^{\gamma_{0}}}\,\frac{1}{|x|^{3}},

and in particular (
E.40
) holds.
(d) For
\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}
we deduce, from (
E.8
),

\displaystyle|\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\lambda}}\tilde{\psi}^{1}|+|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}(\psi^{1}\partial_{x^{\lambda}}\tilde{\psi}^{1})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|H^{1}\partial^{2}_{x^{\lambda}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|+|H^{1}\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\lambda}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|+|(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}H^{1})\partial^{2}_{x^{\lambda}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|h^{1}||\partial^{2}_{x^{\lambda}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|+C|h^{1}||\partial^{3}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\lambda}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|

\displaystyle+C(|h^{1}|+|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}h^{1}|)|\partial^{2}_{x^{\lambda}x^{\mu}}h^{1}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\delta_{h}^{2}\,\frac{(1+|t|)^{2\delta_{h}}}{(1+|t|+|x|)^{2}(1+(|x|-|t|)_{+})^{2\gamma_{0}}},

which is (
E.35
). This completes the proof of Lemma
3.1
.

\Box

Corollary 3.2

We have

|H^{\mu\nu}(x^{0},x)|+\lambda|\partial_{x^{\alpha}}H^{\mu\nu}(x^{0},x)|\leq C\delta_{h}\,\frac{(1+\lambda^{-1}|x^{0}|)^{\delta_{h}}}{1+\lambda^{-1}|x^{0}|+\lambda^{-1}|x|}.

(3.52)

Proof :
This follows from (
3.51
) and (
E.17
)
by re-introducing the constants.

\Box

3.6
Remainders 30 through 37

The bound (
3.47
) just obtained on
({\rm rem}_{29})
immediately implies for
({\rm rem}_{30})
defined in (
2.53
)

\lambda\,|\partial_{x^{\gamma}}{({\rm rem}_{30})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)|\leq\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}},\quad\alpha,\beta,\gamma=0,1,2,3,

(3.53)

which in turn gives for
({\rm rem}_{31})
defined in (
2.56
) (using
c^{-1}\partial_{t}=\partial_{x^{0}}
)

|{({\rm rem}_{31})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)|\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}},\quad\alpha,\beta=0,1,2,3.

(3.54)

Next recall
K_{\ast}
from (
1.26
). If
|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}
and
|x|\geq 2\lambda K_{\ast}
, then

|x-\bar{x}|\geq|x|-|\bar{x}|\geq\frac{1}{2}\,|x|.

Therefore (
3.53
) and
\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}=\eta^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\eta^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}T_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}
from (
2.53
)
together with the boundedness condition (
1.30
) shows that

\displaystyle|\mathchoice{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{({\rm rem}_{32})^{{{\alpha}{\beta}{k}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\,\frac{G}{c^{4}}\,|x|\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|^{3}}\,|\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})|\,d\bar{x}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}

(3.55)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\,\frac{\lambda}{|x|^{2}}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}

for
\alpha,\beta=0,1,2,3
and
k=1,2,3
. We also remark that, since
|ct-|x-\bar{x}||\leq||x-\bar{x}|-|x||+|ct-|x||\leq\lambda(K_{\ast}+1)
, there would be no further gain from this term if some decay of the energy-momentum tensor in terms of
|x^{0}|
were assumed in (
1.30
).

Next we estimate
({\rm rem}_{33})
. An inspection of the algebra prior to its definition in (
2.58
) implies that this term is given by

\displaystyle{({\rm rem}_{33})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{({\rm rem}_{31})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\bigg(\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|}\,\partial_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})\,d\bar{x}-\frac{1}{r}\,\partial_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0001pt-\,\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})}{cr^{2}}\,\partial^{2}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})-\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})^{2}}{2c^{2}r^{3}}\,\partial^{3}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})\bigg)\,d\bar{x},

where
|u|\leq\lambda c^{-1}
and
|x|\geq 5\lambda K_{\ast}
. Hence using (
3.54
),
and by Lemma
5.12
(b) applied pointwise
to
\psi=\partial_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(\cdot,\bar{x})

(and the
\lambda
’s inserted), we obtain for
\alpha,\beta=0,1,2,3
with the additional help of the boundedness condition (
1.30
)

\displaystyle|{({\rm rem}_{33})}^{\alpha\beta}(t,x)|

(3.56)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}+C\,\frac{G\lambda^{3}}{c^{5}}\,r^{-2}\sum_{j=0}^{2}c^{-j}\lambda^{j+1}{\|\partial_{t}^{j+1}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}+C\,\frac{G\lambda^{3}}{c^{5}}\,r^{-1}c^{-3}\lambda^{3}\,{\|\partial_{t}^{4}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}+C\,\frac{G\lambda^{3}}{c^{4}r^{2}}\Big(\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{0}}T_{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial_{x^{0}}^{2}T_{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}+\lambda^{3}{\|\partial_{x^{0}}^{3}T_{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\,C\,\frac{G\lambda^{6}}{c^{4}r}\,{\|\partial_{x^{0}}^{4}T_{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}+C\,\frac{G\lambda^{3}}{c^{4}r^{2}}\Big(\lambda\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{3}}\,\delta_{T,\,\alpha\beta}^{(1),x^{0}}+\lambda^{2}\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{4}}\,\delta_{T,\,\alpha\beta}^{(2),x^{0}}+\lambda^{3}\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{5}}\,\delta_{T,\,\alpha\beta}^{(3),x^{0}}\Big)+C\frac{G\lambda^{6}}{c^{4}r}\,\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{6}}\,\delta_{T,\,\alpha\beta}^{(4),x^{0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}+C\,\frac{\lambda}{r^{2}}\,(\delta_{T}^{(1)}+\delta_{T}^{(2)}+\delta_{T}^{(3)})+C\,\frac{1}{r}\,\delta_{T,\,\alpha\beta}^{(4),x^{0}}

Similarly, the full expression for
({\rm rem}_{34})
given in (
2.59
) is

\displaystyle{({\rm rem}_{34})}^{\alpha\beta k}(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{({\rm rem}_{32})}^{\alpha\beta k}(t,x)-\frac{4G}{c^{5}}\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}x^{k}\bigg(\frac{1}{|x-\bar{x}|^{2}}\,\partial_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}|x-\bar{x}|,\bar{x})-\frac{1}{r^{2}}\,\partial_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})

\displaystyle\hskip 130.0002pt-\,\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})}{cr^{3}}\,\partial^{2}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})-\,\frac{(x\cdot\bar{x})^{2}}{2c^{2}r^{4}}\,\partial^{3}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}(t-c^{-1}r,\bar{x})\bigg)\,d\bar{x}.

In conjunction with (
3.55
) and Lemma
5.12
(c), this leads to the following bound for
\alpha,\beta=0,1,2,3
and
k=1,2,3
:

\displaystyle|{({\rm rem}_{34})}^{\alpha\beta k}(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\,\frac{\lambda}{|x|^{2}}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}

(3.57)

\displaystyle+\,C\,\frac{G\lambda^{3}}{c^{5}}\,|x|\,r^{-3}\sum_{j=0}^{2}c^{-j}\lambda^{j+1}{\|\partial^{j+1}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}+C\,\frac{G\lambda^{3}}{c^{5}}\,|x|\,r^{-2}c^{-3}\lambda^{3}\,{\|\partial^{4}_{t}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\,\frac{\lambda}{|x|^{2}}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}

\displaystyle+\,C\,\frac{G\lambda^{3}}{c^{4}}\,r^{-2}\Big(\lambda{\|\partial_{x^{0}}T_{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}+\lambda^{2}{\|\partial_{x^{0}}^{2}T_{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}+\lambda^{3}{\|\partial_{x^{0}}^{3}T_{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\,C\,\frac{G\lambda^{6}}{c^{4}}\,r^{-1}\,{\|\partial_{x^{0}}^{4}\hat{T}^{\alpha\beta}\|}_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\,\frac{\lambda}{|x|^{2}}\,\delta_{T}^{(0)}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}+C\,\frac{\lambda}{|x|^{2}}\,\,(\delta_{T}^{(1)}+\delta_{T}^{(2)}+\delta_{T}^{(3)})+C\,\frac{1}{|x|}\,\delta_{T,\,\alpha\beta}^{(4),x^{0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}+C\,\frac{\lambda}{|x|^{2}}\,\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{3}\delta_{T}^{(j)}\Big)+C\,\frac{1}{|x|}\,\delta_{T,\,\alpha\beta}^{(4),x^{0}}

3.8
Remainders 43 through 50

These remainders, introduced in Subsection
2.6
, ultimately depend on remainders 10 and 38–42, as well as on the quadrupole moment
D
. The latter was defined in (
1.16
) as

D^{\alpha\beta}(u)=\frac{1}{c^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}T_{00}(u,\bar{x})\,\bar{x}^{\alpha}\,\bar{x}^{\beta}\,d\bar{x}.

We will need to know a bound for its triple derivative:

\dddot{D}^{\alpha\beta}(u)=c\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{3}_{x^{0}}T_{00}(u,\bar{x})\,\bar{x}^{\alpha}\,\bar{x}^{\beta}\,d\bar{x},

where
u=t-c^{-1}r
satisfies by hypothesis
|u|\leq\lambda c^{-1}
. As always, the integration domain may be reduced to

\dddot{D}^{\alpha\beta}(u)=c\int_{|\bar{x}|\leq\lambda K_{\ast}}\partial^{3}_{x^{0}}T_{00}(u,\bar{x})\,\bar{x}^{\alpha}\,\bar{x}^{\beta}\,d\bar{x}

and from the boundedness condition (
1.30
) we immediately have, for
\alpha,\beta=0,1,2,3
,

|\dddot{D}^{\alpha\beta}(u)|\leq C\,c\lambda^{5}\,\frac{c^{4}}{G\lambda^{5}}\,\delta_{T,\,00}^{(3),x^{0}}=C\,\frac{c^{5}}{G}\,\delta_{T,\,00}^{(3),x^{0}}

(3.66)

Using (
3.7
) and (
3.62
) we deduce from (
2.83
) that

\displaystyle|({\rm rem}_{43})(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\,\frac{C\delta_{h}^{2}}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{\gamma_{0}-\delta_{h}}}+C\,\frac{\lambda}{|x|^{2}}\,(\delta_{T}^{(1)}+\delta_{T}^{(2)}+\delta_{T}^{(3)})

(3.67)

\displaystyle+\,C\,\frac{1}{|x|}\,\Big(\delta_{T,\,00}^{(4),x^{0}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\delta_{T,\,jj}^{(2),x^{0}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\delta_{T,\,jj}^{(3),x^{0}}+\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{3}\delta_{T}^{(j)}\Big)\delta_{h}\Big).

\displaystyle\hskip 30.00005pt\times\Big[(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{1-\gamma_{0}+\delta_{h}}\,\delta_{h}^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{|x|}\,\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{3}\delta_{T}^{(j)}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt+\,\Big(\delta_{T,\,00}^{(4),x^{0}}+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\delta_{T,\,ij}^{(4),x^{0}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\delta_{T,\,0j}^{(4),x^{0}}

\displaystyle\hskip 70.0001pt+\,\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\delta_{T,\,ij}^{(3),x^{0}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\delta_{T,\,0j}^{(3),x^{0}}+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\delta_{T,\,ij}^{(2),x^{0}}+\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{3}\delta_{T}^{(j)}\Big)\delta_{h}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\,C\,\frac{c^{5}}{G}\,\frac{1}{(\lambda^{-1}|x|)^{1-3\delta_{h}}}\,\delta_{h}^{3}.

This concludes the proof of Theorem
1.2
.

Appendix A
Appendix: The energy-momentum pseudo tensor

The Landau-Lifschitz energy-momentum pseudo tensor
t^{\alpha\beta}
,
see
[
14
, Section 28.2]
or
[
10
, equ. (20.22)]
, is given by

\displaystyle\frac{16\pi G}{c^{4}}\,|g|\,t^{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\delta}}\mathfrak{g}^{\delta\gamma}-\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\alpha\gamma}\,\partial_{x^{\delta}}\mathfrak{g}^{\beta\delta}+\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\beta}\,g_{\delta\varepsilon}\,\partial_{x^{\sigma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\delta\gamma}\,\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\sigma\varepsilon}

(A.1)

\displaystyle-g_{\varepsilon\gamma}\,\partial_{x^{\delta}}\mathfrak{g}^{\varepsilon\sigma}\,(g^{\alpha\delta}\,\partial_{x^{\sigma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\beta\gamma}+g^{\beta\delta}\,\partial_{x^{\sigma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\alpha\gamma})+g_{\delta\varepsilon}\,g^{\gamma\sigma}\,\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\beta\delta}\,\partial_{x^{\sigma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\alpha\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\,(2g^{\alpha\delta}\,g^{\beta\gamma}-g^{\alpha\beta}\,g^{\delta\gamma})(2g_{\varepsilon\sigma}\,g_{\kappa\nu}-g_{\sigma\kappa}\,g_{\varepsilon\nu})\,\partial_{x^{\delta}}\mathfrak{g}^{\varepsilon\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\gamma}}\mathfrak{g}^{\sigma\kappa},

where

\mathfrak{g}^{\alpha\beta}=|g|^{1/2}\,g^{\alpha\beta}.

(A.2)

It is not a tensor; cf. for instance
[
14
, p. 233]
. Defining also the quantities

U^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta}=\frac{1}{2}\,|g|\,(g^{\alpha\beta}\,g^{\gamma\delta}-g^{\alpha\gamma}\,g^{\beta\delta})\quad\mbox{and}\quad U^{\alpha\beta\gamma}=\partial_{x^{\delta}}U^{\alpha\beta\gamma\delta},

(the latter being sometimes called a superpotential), we immediately have

|g|\,\Big(G^{\alpha\beta}+\frac{8\pi G}{c^{4}}\,t^{\alpha\beta}\Big)=\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha\beta\gamma},\quad U^{\alpha\beta\gamma}=-U^{\alpha\gamma\beta},\quad\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha\beta\gamma}=\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{\beta\alpha\gamma},

(A.3)

from which it follows that

\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha\beta\gamma}=0.

(A.4)

Consequently,

\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Big[|g|\,\Big(G^{\alpha\beta}+\frac{8\pi G}{c^{4}}\,t^{\alpha\beta}\Big)\Big]=0,

which together with the Einstein equations (
1.1
) implies the conservation law

\partial_{x^{\beta}}[\,|g|\,(T^{\alpha\beta}+t^{\alpha\beta})]=0.

(A.5)

This has to be contrasted to the identity
\nabla_{\beta}T^{\alpha\beta}=0

in terms of covariant derivatives. Equation (
A.5
) implies that the quantities

|g|(T^{\alpha\beta}+t^{\alpha\beta})
can be viewed as components of a fictitious energy-momentum tensor
of the matter system coupled to gravity, and in particular the quantities

\mathfrak{p}^{\alpha}(t,x)=\int_{B_{r}(0)}|g(t,\bar{x})|\,(T^{0\alpha}(t,\bar{x})+t^{0\alpha}(t,\bar{x}))\,d\bar{x},\quad\alpha=0,1,2,3,

represent the total energy (for
\alpha=0
) or
j
-momentum (for
\alpha=j
) present inside
a ball of radius
r=|x|
at time
t
. Here
d\bar{x}=d\bar{x}^{1}\,d\bar{x}^{2}\,d\bar{x}^{3}
.

Now note that (
A.4
) also implies

0=\partial^{2}_{x^{\beta}x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha\beta\gamma}=\partial^{2}_{x^{0}x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha 0\gamma}+\partial^{2}_{x^{i}x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha i\gamma},

which upon integration yields

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{0}}\int_{B_{r}(0)}\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha 0\gamma}\,d\bar{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{B_{r}(0)}\partial^{2}_{x^{i}x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha i\gamma}\,d\bar{x}

(A.6)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\int_{\partial B_{r}(0)}\frac{\bar{x}^{i}}{r}\,\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha i\gamma}\,dS(\bar{x}),

where we have used Gauss’s Theorem in the last step, with
dS(\bar{x})=dS(\bar{x}^{1},\bar{x}^{2},\bar{x}^{3})
representing the surface measure on
\partial B_{r}(0)
.
We have suppressed the function arguments
(t,\bar{x})
inside the integrals, but we remark that

|x|=|\bar{x}|=r
for any integral performed over
\partial B_{r}(0)
. From the first and third relation
in (
A.3
) it similarly follows that

\mathfrak{p}^{\alpha}=\frac{c^{4}}{8\pi G}\int_{B_{r}(0)}\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{0\alpha\gamma}\,d\bar{x}=\frac{c^{4}}{8\pi G}\int_{B_{r}(0)}\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha 0\gamma}\,d\bar{x}.

(A.7)

Also (
A.3
) yields
U^{\alpha\beta\beta}=0
for all
\alpha,\beta
, which we use to simplify (
A.7
) into

\mathfrak{p}^{\alpha}=\frac{c^{4}}{8\pi G}\int_{B_{r}(0)}\partial_{x^{i}}U^{\alpha 0i}\,d\bar{x}=\frac{c^{4}}{8\pi G}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\int_{\partial B_{r}(0)}\frac{\bar{x}^{i}}{r}\,U^{\alpha 0i}\,dS(\bar{x}),

using Gauss’s Theorem one more time. Because of
\partial_{x^{0}}=c^{-1}\partial_{t}
,
equations (
A.7
), (
A.6
) and (
A.3
) imply the following expression for the rate of change
of the total energy inside
B_{r}(0)
:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\,\mathfrak{p}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c\,\partial_{x^{0}}\,\mathfrak{p}^{\alpha}

(A.8)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{c^{5}}{8\pi G}\,\partial_{x^{0}}\int_{B_{r}(0)}\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha 0\gamma}\,d\bar{x}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{c^{5}}{8\pi G}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\int_{\partial B_{r}(0)}\frac{\bar{x}^{i}}{r}\,\partial_{x^{\gamma}}U^{\alpha i\gamma}\,dS(\bar{x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-c\sum_{i=1}^{3}\int_{\partial B_{r}(0)}|g|\,\frac{\bar{x}^{i}}{r}\,(T^{\alpha i}+t^{\alpha i})\,dS(\bar{x}).

Thus (
1.14
) in the introduction is justified.

Appendix B
Appendix: The harmonic gauge

In this section, for the sake of making this work self contained, we provide some well known facts related to the harmonic gauge. We follow
[
16
, p. 162]
.

The harmonic-gauge condition is

\Gamma^{\lambda}:=g^{\mu\nu}\Gamma^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}=0.

(B.1)

Lemma 2.1

(a) One has
\Gamma^{\lambda}=-|g|^{-1/2}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}(|g|^{1/2}g^{\lambda\kappa})
.
In particular, (
B.1
) is equivalent to
the relation
\partial_{x^{\kappa}}(|g|^{1/2}g^{\lambda\kappa})=0
.

(b) One has
\Gamma^{\lambda}=-\Box_{g}x^{\lambda}
, where
\Box_{g}\phi=\nabla_{\mu}\nabla^{\mu}\phi=\nabla_{\mu}(g^{\nu\mu}\nabla_{\nu}\phi)
. In particular, (
B.1
) is equivalent
to the relation
\Box_{g}x^{\lambda}=0
.

(c) The condition (
B.1
) is also equivalent to

g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\nu\lambda}=\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\lambda}}g_{\mu\nu}

(B.2)

and furthermore to

\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\mu\lambda}=\frac{1}{2}\,g_{\mu\nu}\,g^{\lambda\kappa}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g^{\mu\nu}.

(B.3)

Proof :
(a) Since

\Gamma^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}=\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\lambda\kappa}(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\kappa\mu}+\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\kappa\nu}-\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}),

one has

\Gamma^{\lambda}=\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\kappa\mu}+\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\kappa\nu}-\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}).

(B.4)

First note that
g^{\lambda\kappa}g_{\kappa\mu}=\delta^{\lambda}_{\nu}
yields upon differentiation

\partial_{x^{\nu}}
that

g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\kappa\mu}=-g_{\kappa\mu}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g^{\lambda\kappa}.

(B.5)

Next, in general the determinant function
\det:\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}\to\mathbb{R}
is differentiable
at any invertible matrix
M
and such that
D\det(M)(A)=\det M\,{\rm tr}\,(M^{-1}A)
. Thus if
M=M(x)
is
x
-dependent, we get

\partial_{x^{\mu}}\det M(x)=\det M(x)\,{\rm tr}\,(M^{-1}(x)\partial_{x^{\mu}}M(x)).

Taking for
M=(g_{\mu\nu})
the metric tensor, this yields for
g=\det\,(g_{\mu\nu})
the relation

g^{-1}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g={\rm tr}\,((g^{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}})\,(\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}))=g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}.

Since
g<0
this may be rewritten as

|g|^{-1}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}|g|=g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}.

(B.6)

Together with (
B.5
) we arrive at

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\kappa\mu}+\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\kappa\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}

(B.7)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}g_{\kappa\mu}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g^{\lambda\kappa}-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}g_{\kappa\nu}\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\lambda\kappa}-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\lambda\kappa}|g|^{-1}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}|g|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g^{\lambda\kappa}-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\lambda\kappa}|g|^{-1}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}|g|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-|g|^{-1/2}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}(|g|^{1/2}g^{\lambda\kappa}),

as claimed. (b) To make a connection to
\Box_{g}
, we assert that in general

\Box_{g}\phi=g^{\nu\mu}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}\phi-\Gamma^{\nu}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\phi.

(B.8)

To establish (
B.8
), the first thing to observe is that
\nabla_{\nu}\phi=\partial_{x^{\nu}}\phi
,
since
\phi
is a scalar. Let
(V^{\mu})
be a contravariant vector. From

\Gamma^{\mu}_{\mu\nu}=\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\kappa}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\kappa\nu}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\kappa\mu}-\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu})=\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\kappa}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\mu\kappa}

and (
B.6
) we obtain

\Gamma^{\mu}_{\mu\nu}=\frac{1}{2}\,|g|^{-1}\partial_{x^{\nu}}|g|=|g|^{-1/2}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}|g|^{1/2}.

This in turn yields

\nabla_{\mu}V^{\mu}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}V^{\mu}+\Gamma^{\mu}_{\mu\nu}V^{\nu}=|g|^{-1/2}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}(|g|^{1/2}\,V^{\mu})

for the covariant divergence of a contravariant vector.
Altogether, and using once again (
B.7
),

\displaystyle\Box_{g}\phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu}(g^{\nu\mu}\nabla_{\nu}\phi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mu}(g^{\nu\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}\phi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|g|^{-1/2}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}(|g|^{1/2}\,g^{\nu\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}\phi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\nu\mu}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}\phi+|g|^{-1/2}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}(|g|^{1/2}\,g^{\nu\mu})\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}\phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\nu\mu}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}\phi-\Gamma^{\nu}\partial_{x^{\nu}}\phi,

which completes the proof of (
B.8
). Since
\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}x^{\lambda}=0

for all
\nu,\mu,\lambda
, this specializes to
\Box_{g}x^{\lambda}=-\Gamma^{\lambda}
,
so that the claim follows.
(c) First note that by definition

\displaystyle 2\Gamma^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\kappa\mu}+\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\kappa\nu}-\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial^{\mu}g_{\kappa\mu}+g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial^{\nu}g_{\kappa\nu}-g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial^{\mu}g_{\kappa\mu}-g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu},

which means that

\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}=g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial^{\mu}g_{\kappa\mu}-\Gamma^{\lambda}.

(B.9)

Hence

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\lambda}}g_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}\,\delta^{\kappa}_{\lambda}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}=\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}\,g_{\lambda\sigma}\,g^{\sigma\kappa}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}=g_{\lambda\sigma}\,(g^{\sigma\kappa}\partial^{\mu}g_{\kappa\mu}-\Gamma^{\sigma})

(B.10)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{\lambda}^{\kappa}\,\partial^{\mu}g_{\kappa\mu}-g_{\lambda\sigma}\Gamma^{\sigma}=\partial^{\mu}g_{\lambda\mu}-\Gamma_{\lambda}=g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\nu\lambda}-\Gamma_{\lambda}.

To prove the equivalence of (
B.1
), (
B.2
) and (
B.3
),
suppose first that (
B.1
) holds. Then, due to (
B.10
),

\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\lambda}}g_{\mu\nu}=g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\nu\lambda},

which is (
B.2
). If (
B.2
) is verified,
then we may use (
B.5
) twice to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g_{\mu\nu}\,g^{\lambda\kappa}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\lambda\kappa}\,g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}=-g^{\lambda\kappa}\,g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\nu\kappa}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\kappa}\,g_{\nu\kappa}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\mu\nu}=\delta_{\nu}^{\lambda}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\mu\nu}=\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\mu\lambda},

and hence (
B.3
) is valid. Lastly, suppose that (
B.3
) holds.
Then, once again by (
B.5
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\lambda\kappa}g^{\mu\nu}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g_{\mu\nu}=-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\lambda\kappa}g_{\mu\nu}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g^{\mu\nu}=-\frac{1}{2}\,g_{\mu\nu}g^{\lambda\kappa}\partial_{x^{\kappa}}g^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\mu\lambda}=-g_{\kappa\mu}\,\partial^{\kappa}g^{\mu\lambda}=g^{\mu\lambda}\,\partial^{\kappa}g_{\kappa\mu},

but according to (
B.9
) this implies (
B.1
).

\Box

Next we rewrite the Ricci tensor, following
[
8
, Lemma 3.1]
. First introduce

\Gamma_{\mu\alpha\nu}=g_{\alpha\lambda}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}.

Then

\displaystyle\Gamma_{\mu\alpha\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g_{\alpha\lambda}\,g^{\lambda\delta}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\nu\delta}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\mu\delta}-\partial_{x^{\delta}}g_{\mu\nu})

(B.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\nu\alpha}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\mu\alpha}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\mu\nu}),

and in particular

\Gamma_{\beta\lambda\alpha}=\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\lambda}+\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\lambda}-\partial_{x^{\lambda}}g_{\beta\alpha})=\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\lambda}+\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\lambda}-\partial_{x^{\lambda}}g_{\alpha\beta})=\Gamma_{\alpha\lambda\beta}.

Furthermore,

\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}=\Gamma_{\alpha\beta\mu}+\Gamma_{\alpha\mu\beta}.

In fact,

\displaystyle\Gamma_{\alpha\beta\mu}+\Gamma_{\alpha\mu\beta}=\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\mu\beta}+\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}-\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\mu})+\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}+\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\mu}-\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta})=\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}.

This in turn implies that

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\beta}}(g_{\alpha\lambda}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda})=g_{\alpha\lambda}(\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda})+(\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\lambda})\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{\alpha\lambda}(\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda})+(\Gamma_{\beta\alpha\lambda}+\Gamma_{\beta\lambda\alpha})\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}.

The Riemann curvature tensor we will use in the form

\displaystyle\mathchoice{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\alpha}{\nu}{\beta}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\alpha}{\nu}{\beta}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\alpha}{\nu}{\beta}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\alpha}{\nu}{\beta}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{\alpha\lambda}\,\mathchoice{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\beta}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\beta}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\beta}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\beta}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{\alpha\lambda}\,(\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Gamma^{\lambda}_{\mu\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma^{\lambda}_{\mu\beta}+\Gamma^{\lambda}_{\gamma\beta}\,\Gamma^{\gamma}_{\mu\nu}-\Gamma^{\lambda}_{\gamma\nu}\,\Gamma^{\gamma}_{\mu\beta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha\nu}-(\Gamma_{\beta\alpha\lambda}+\Gamma_{\beta\lambda\alpha})\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha\beta}+(\Gamma_{\nu\alpha\lambda}+\Gamma_{\nu\lambda\alpha})\Gamma_{\mu\beta}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle+\,\Gamma_{\gamma\alpha\beta}\,\Gamma^{\gamma}_{\mu\nu}-\Gamma_{\gamma\alpha\nu}\,\Gamma^{\gamma}_{\mu\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha\beta}+\Gamma_{\nu\lambda\alpha}\Gamma_{\mu\beta}^{\lambda}-\Gamma_{\alpha\lambda\beta}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}.

For the Ricci tensor, we consequently obtain

R_{\mu\nu}=\mathchoice{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\alpha}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\alpha}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\alpha}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}{\alpha}}}}=g^{\alpha\beta}\,\mathchoice{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\beta}{\nu}{\alpha}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\beta}{\nu}{\alpha}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\beta}{\nu}{\alpha}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\beta}{\nu}{\alpha}}}}=g^{\alpha\beta}\,(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\alpha})+g^{\alpha\beta}\,(\Gamma_{\nu\lambda\beta}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha}^{\lambda}-\Gamma_{\beta\lambda\alpha}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}).

(B.12)

We look at the different parts on the right-hand side of (
B.12
) separately.
To begin with,

g^{\alpha\beta}\,\Gamma_{\beta\lambda\alpha}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}=g^{\alpha\beta}\,\Gamma_{\alpha\lambda\beta}\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}=g^{\alpha\beta}\,g_{\lambda\gamma}\,\Gamma_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma}\,\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}=g_{\lambda\gamma}\,\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}\,\Gamma^{\gamma}=\Gamma_{\mu\nu}^{\lambda}\,\Gamma_{\lambda}.

(B.13)

Next, by (
B.11
) and through a renaming of indices

\lambda\leftrightarrow\alpha
, and thereafter
\lambda\leftrightarrow\beta^{\prime}
,

\gamma\leftrightarrow\alpha^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}\,\Gamma_{\nu\lambda\beta}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha\beta}\,(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\lambda}+\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\nu\lambda}-\partial_{x^{\lambda}}g_{\nu\beta})\,g^{\lambda\gamma}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\gamma}+\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\mu\gamma}-\partial_{x^{\gamma}}g_{\mu\alpha})

(B.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\alpha}+\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\nu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu})\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}+\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\mu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}).\qquad

To proceed further, note that by exchanging

\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta^{\prime}
,
\beta\leftrightarrow\alpha^{\prime}
,

g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,(\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\nu})\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\mu}=g^{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}g^{\beta^{\prime}\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu},

(B.15)

and moreover from
\beta\leftrightarrow\beta^{\prime}
,
\alpha\leftrightarrow\alpha^{\prime}
,

g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}=g^{\beta^{\prime}\beta}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}.

(B.16)

Similarly,
\beta\leftrightarrow\beta^{\prime}
,
\alpha\leftrightarrow\alpha^{\prime}
, yields

g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}=g^{\beta^{\prime}\beta}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}

(B.17)

and
\beta\leftrightarrow\alpha^{\prime}
,
\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta^{\prime}
, leads to

g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\mu}=g^{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}g^{\beta^{\prime}\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}.

(B.18)

Furthermore,
\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
,
\alpha^{\prime}\leftrightarrow\beta^{\prime}
implies

g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\mu}=g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}

(B.19)

as well as

g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}=g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}.

(B.20)

Using (
B.15
)-(
B.20
) in (
B.14
), it follows that

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}\,\Gamma_{\nu\lambda\beta}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\mu}-\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\mu}-\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\mu}+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}-\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\mu}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}g^{\beta^{\prime}\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}-\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta^{\prime}\beta}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}g^{\beta^{\prime}\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\beta^{\prime}\beta}g^{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\nu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\bigg(\frac{1}{4}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\bigg).

This is further rewritten as

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}\,\Gamma_{\nu\lambda\beta}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha}^{\lambda}

(B.21)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\bigg(\frac{1}{4}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\bigg)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\bigg(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}-\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\bigg).

The next step is to observe that, by (
B.10
),

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\mu})\,(g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}+\Gamma_{\mu}\bigg)\,\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}+\Gamma_{\nu}\bigg).

Going back to (
B.21
), we arrive at

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}\,\Gamma_{\nu\lambda\beta}\Gamma_{\mu\alpha}^{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\bigg(\frac{1}{4}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha^{\prime}}}g_{\beta^{\prime}\nu}\bigg)

(B.22)

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2}\,\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}+\Gamma_{\mu}\bigg)\,\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}+\Gamma_{\nu}\bigg)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\bigg(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}-\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\mu}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\bigg).

Lastly, we look at the contribution of
g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\alpha})

to (
B.12
), which contains the ‘principal part’. From (
B.11
) we find

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\alpha})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\mu}}g_{\nu\beta}+\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\nu}}g_{\mu\beta}-\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}}g_{\mu\nu})

(B.23)

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}+\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\alpha}}g_{\mu\beta}-\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\beta}}g_{\mu\alpha})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,\Box^{\prime}_{g}\,g_{\mu\nu}+\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}+\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\beta}}g_{\mu\alpha}-\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,\Box^{\prime}_{g}\,g_{\mu\nu}+\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\beta}\bigg(\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\beta}\bigg(\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\beta}}g_{\mu\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg),

with the operator
\Box^{\prime}_{g}=g^{\alpha\beta}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\beta}}
;
note that
\Box^{\prime}_{g}=\Box_{g}
in the harmonic gauge due to (
B.8
).
From (
B.10
) we get

g^{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta}-\Gamma_{\nu}=0,

and differentiation
\partial_{x^{\mu}}
yields

g^{\alpha\beta}\bigg(\partial^{2}_{x^{\alpha}x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial^{2}_{x^{\nu}x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg)=-\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\alpha\beta}\bigg(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg)+\partial_{x^{\mu}}\Gamma_{\nu}.

Using this, and the same relation for

\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
,
\mu\leftrightarrow\nu
,
(
B.23
) leads to

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\alpha})

(B.24)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,\Box^{\prime}_{g}\,g_{\mu\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\alpha\beta}\bigg(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg)+\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}\Gamma_{\nu}

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g^{\alpha\beta}\bigg(\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\mu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg)+\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{x^{\mu}}(g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}})g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}+g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}})g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}+g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\alpha\beta}+g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})

shows that

\partial_{x^{\mu}}g^{\alpha\beta}=-g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}).

Using this, and the same relation for

\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
,
\mu\leftrightarrow\nu
,
we can continue (
B.24
) as

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\alpha})

(B.25)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,\Box^{\prime}_{g}\,g_{\mu\nu}+\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\bigg(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\beta\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\bigg(\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\mu}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg)+\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}\Gamma_{\nu}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu}).

Due to
\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
we get

g^{\beta\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\,(\partial_{x^{\beta}}g_{\alpha\mu})=g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\,(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu})

(B.26)

as well as

g^{\beta\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\alpha\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta})=g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}),

(B.27)

and
\alpha\leftrightarrow\alpha^{\prime}
,
\beta\leftrightarrow\beta^{\prime}
gives

g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}})\,(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\beta})=g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta})\,(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}).

(B.28)

From (
B.26
)-(
B.28
) applied in (
B.25
) we deduce

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\alpha})

(B.29)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,\Box^{\prime}_{g}\,g_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}\Gamma_{\nu}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu}).

To re-express the middle term on the right-hand side,
we invoke (
B.10
) twice to obtain

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}+g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}+\Gamma^{\beta}\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}

\displaystyle+\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\nu}

\displaystyle+\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\nu})+(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu})\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\Gamma^{\beta}\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\nu})+(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu})\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\Gamma^{\beta}\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}+\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\Gamma_{\nu}.

From this expression, and the same with
\mu\leftrightarrow\nu
,
in (
B.29
), we get

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}(\partial_{x^{\alpha}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu\beta\alpha})

(B.30)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,\Box^{\prime}_{g}\,g_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{8}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\nu})+(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu})\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\,\Gamma^{\beta}\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\Gamma_{\nu}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\mu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\mu})+(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\mu})\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\,\Gamma^{\beta}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\mu}+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\Gamma_{\mu}

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}\Gamma_{\nu}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,\Box^{\prime}_{g}\,g_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle+\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\bigg(\frac{1}{4}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\beta}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big((\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\nu})+(\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\mu}-\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\alpha}\,\partial_{x^{\beta^{\prime}}}g_{\beta\mu})\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}g^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Big((\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\nu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu})+(\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\beta\mu}-\partial_{x^{\alpha}}g_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\,\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\mu})\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\,(\partial_{x^{\mu}}\Gamma_{\nu}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}\Gamma_{\mu})+\frac{1}{2}\,\Gamma^{\beta}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\beta\nu}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\beta\mu})+\frac{1}{4}\,g^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}(\partial_{x^{\mu}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\Gamma_{\nu}+\partial_{x^{\nu}}g_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,\Gamma_{\mu}).

Summarizing (
B.12
), (
B.30
), (
B.22
) and (
B.13
),
we finally obtain

\displaystyle R_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=
